### [1] [CREDO: Epistemic-Aware Conformalized Credal Envelopes for Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06826)
*Luben M. C. Cabezas,Sabina J. Sloman,Bruno M. Resende,Fanyi Wu,Michele Caprio,Rafael Izbicki*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: CREDO是一种结合可信集方法和保形预测的新方法，通过"先可信集后保形化"的流程，在保证边际覆盖率的同时提供可解释的预测区间，将区间宽度分解为偶然噪声、认知不确定性和校准松弛三个部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统保形预测能提供分布无关的覆盖率保证，但在模型外推区域可能显得过于自信，因为标准保形分数不显式表示认知不确定性。而可信集方法虽然能通过一组可信预测分布来体现认知效应，但通常缺乏校准保证。需要结合两者优势的方法。

Method: CREDO采用"先可信集后保形化"的两步流程：1) 构建可解释的可信包络，在局部证据弱时扩大区间；2) 在此包络上应用分割保形校准，保证边际覆盖率。基于修剪极端后验预测端点实现快速计算。

Result: 在基准回归任务中，CREDO在保持目标覆盖率的同时，以竞争性效率提高了稀疏自适应性。预测区间宽度可分解为三个可解释部分：偶然噪声、认知膨胀和分布无关的校准松弛。

Conclusion: CREDO成功结合了可信集方法的可解释性和保形预测的校准保证，提供既可靠又可解释的预测区间，在不确定性量化方面实现了理论保证与实践效果的平衡。

Abstract: Conformal prediction delivers prediction intervals with distribution-free coverage, but its intervals can look overconfident in regions where the model is extrapolating, because standard conformal scores do not explicitly represent epistemic uncertainty. Credal methods, by contrast, make epistemic effects visible by working with sets of plausible predictive distributions, but they are typically model-based and lack calibration guarantees. We introduce CREDO, a simple "credal-then-conformalize" recipe that combines both strengths. CREDO first builds an interpretable credal envelope that widens when local evidence is weak, then applies split conformal calibration on top of this envelope to guarantee marginal coverage without further assumptions. This separation of roles yields prediction intervals that are interpretable: their width can be decomposed into aleatoric noise, epistemic inflation, and a distribution-free calibration slack. We provide a fast implementation based on trimming extreme posterior predictive endpoints, prove validity, and show on benchmark regressions that CREDO maintains target coverage while improving sparsity adaptivity at competitive efficiency.

</details>


### [2] [Bilateral Trade Under Heavy-Tailed Valuations: Minimax Regret with Infinite Variance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06851)
*Hangyi Zhao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文研究了具有有界密度但无限方差估值的情境双边贸易问题，在完全反馈下建立了精确的极小极大后悔率，填补了p∈(1,2)时的理论空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究情境双边贸易中估值具有有界密度但无限方差（即p∈(1,2)的有限矩）的情况，填补现有理论在p=2（有界方差）和p=1（平凡线性率）之间的空白。

Method: 1. 将Bachoc等人的自界性质从有界估值扩展到实值估值；2. 结合截断均值估计；3. 设计基于周期的算法；4. 使用Assouad方法和平滑矩匹配构造建立下界。

Result: 1. 证明了任何价格π的期望后悔满足E[g(m,V,W)-g(π,V,W)] ≤ L|m-π|²；2. 算法获得后悔率Õ(T^{1-2β(p-1)/(βp+d(p-1))})；3. 建立了匹配的Ω(·)下界；4. 刻画了该问题的精确极小极大率。

Conclusion: 该研究完全刻画了情境双边贸易在估值具有有界密度但无限方差时的极小极大后悔率，结果在p=2时退化为经典非参数率，在p→1⁺时退化为平凡线性率，填补了理论空白。

Abstract: We study contextual bilateral trade under full feedback when trader valuations have bounded density but infinite variance. We first extend the self-bounding property of Bachoc et al. (ICML 2025) from bounded to real-valued valuations, showing that the expected regret of any price $π$ satisfies $\mathbb{E}[g(m,V,W) - g(π,V,W)] \le L|m-π|^2$ under bounded density alone. Combining this with truncated-mean estimation, we prove that an epoch-based algorithm achieves regret $\widetilde{O}(T^{1-2β(p-1)/(βp + d(p-1))})$ when the noise has finite $p$-th moment for $p \in (1,2)$ and the market value function is $β$-Hölder, and we establish a matching $Ω(\cdot)$ lower bound via Assouad's method with a smoothed moment-matching construction. Our results characterize the exact minimax rate for this problem, interpolating between the classical nonparametric rate at $p=2$ and the trivial linear rate as $p \to 1^+$.

</details>


### [3] [Fairness May Backfire: When Leveling-Down Occurs in Fair Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06901)
*Yi Yang,Xiangyu Chang,Pei-yu Chen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该研究在贝叶斯框架下分析公平性约束对机器学习分类器的影响，发现在属性感知决策中公平性必然改善弱势群体结果，而在属性盲决策中影响取决于数据分布，可能导致"向下拉平"或"向上提升"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器学习系统在信贷、就业等关键领域广泛应用，算法公平性成为核心关注点。但目前不清楚实施公平性约束是真正改善了受影响群体的结果，还是导致了"向下拉平"（使一个或两个群体都变得更差）。

Method: 采用统一的群体层面贝叶斯框架分析二元分类中的公平性概念。该方法具有分布无关性和算法无关性，能够将公平性要求的内在效应与有限样本噪声、训练和干预细节分离开来。分析了两种部署机制：属性感知决策（决策时可用敏感属性）和属性盲决策（预测时排除敏感属性）。

Result: 在属性感知机制中，公平机器学习必然（弱）改善弱势群体的结果，同时（弱）恶化优势群体的结果。在属性盲机制中，公平性的影响取决于数据分布：公平性可能使任一群体受益或受损，甚至可能使两个群体的结果向同一方向移动，导致"向上提升"或"向下拉平"。研究还揭示了"被掩盖"候选人在驱动这些模式中的作用。

Conclusion: 研究结果为何时追求算法公平性可能改善群体结果、何时可能导致系统性"向下拉平"提供了结构性指导，为公平机器学习的设计和部署选择提供了信息。强调了决策机制（属性感知vs属性盲）对公平性实施效果的关键影响。

Abstract: As machine learning (ML) systems increasingly shape access to credit, jobs, and other opportunities, the fairness of algorithmic decisions has become a central concern. Yet it remains unclear when enforcing fairness constraints in these systems genuinely improves outcomes for affected groups or instead leads to "leveling down," making one or both groups worse off. We address this question in a unified, population-level (Bayes) framework for binary classification under prevalent group fairness notions. Our Bayes approach is distribution-free and algorithm-agnostic, isolating the intrinsic effect of fairness requirements from finite-sample noise and from training and intervention specifics. We analyze two deployment regimes for ML classifiers under common legal and governance constraints: attribute-aware decision-making (sensitive attributes available at decision time) and attribute-blind decision-making (sensitive attributes excluded from prediction). We show that, in the attribute-aware regime, fair ML necessarily (weakly) improves outcomes for the disadvantaged group and (weakly) worsens outcomes for the advantaged group. In contrast, in the attribute-blind regime, the impact of fairness is distribution-dependent: fairness can benefit or harm either group and may shift both groups' outcomes in the same direction, leading to either leveling up or leveling down. We characterize the conditions under which these patterns arise and highlight the role of "masked" candidates in driving them. Overall, our results provide structural guidance on when pursuing algorithmic fairness is likely to improve group outcomes and when it risks systemic leveling down, informing fair ML design and deployment choices.

</details>


### [4] [Post-Training with Policy Gradients: Optimality and the Base Model Barrier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06957)
*Alireza Mousavi-Hosseini,Murat A. Erdogdu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用结果奖励和过程奖励对线性自回归模型进行后训练的方法，证明了在基础模型具有非平凡似然的情况下，策略梯度变体可以实现接近最优的奖励查询次数，但存在超越基础模型支持范围的障碍，并提出了使用过程奖励模型来克服这一维度诅咒的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究如何通过后训练改进线性自回归模型的性能，特别是在使用结果奖励和过程奖励的情况下，探索超越基础模型支持范围的可行性，并解决在序列预测中面临的维度诅咒问题。

Method: 采用策略梯度（PG）变体进行后训练，研究两种设置：1）使用结果奖励模型，2）使用过程奖励模型。在统计学习中使用自适应学习率的SGD，在线学习中使用自适应学习率的PG。通过似然分位数（LQ）分析基础模型特性，并证明在边缘条件下这些方法的有效性。

Result: 证明了在基础模型达到非平凡似然α的测试样本上，PG变体可以用接近最优的奖励查询次数实现1-ε的似然。但发现超越基础模型支持范围存在障碍，整体期望误差受基础模型的似然分位数控制。使用过程奖励模型可以避免维度诅咒，依赖token级别的LQ。

Conclusion: 后训练线性自回归模型时，使用结果奖励存在超越基础模型支持范围的限制，而过程奖励模型可以克服维度诅咒问题。在边缘条件下，自适应学习率的SGD和PG分别实现了统计学习和在线学习的接近最优性能，这些发现具有独立的研究价值。

Abstract: We study post-training linear autoregressive models with outcome and process rewards. Given a context $\boldsymbol{x}$, the model must predict the response $\boldsymbol{y} \in Y^N$, a sequence of length $N$ that satisfies a $γ$ margin condition, an extension of the standard separability to sequences. We prove that on test samples where the base model achieves a non-trivial likelihood $α$, a variant of policy gradient (PG) can achieve likelihood $1 - \varepsilon$ with an essentially minimax optimal number of reward queries $\tilde{O}((α^{-1} + \varepsilon^{-1})/γ^2)$. However, a barrier arises for going beyond the support of the base model. We prove that the overall expected error after post-training with outcome rewards is governed by a property of the base model called the Likelihood Quantile (LQ), and that variants of PG, while minimax optimal, may require a number of reward queries exponential in $N$ to go beyond this support, regardless of the pre-training algorithm. To overcome this barrier, we study post-training with a process reward model, and demonstrate how PG variants in this setting avoid the curse of dimensionality in $N$ via dependence on a token-level LQ. Along the way, we prove that under the margin condition, SGD with adaptive learning rate (LR) achieves a near optimal test error for statistical learning, and PG with adaptive LR achieves a near optimal number of mistakes for online learning while being computationally efficient whenever possible, both of which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [5] [Masked Unfairness: Hiding Causality within Zero ATE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06984)
*Zou Yang,Sophia Xiao,Bijan Mazaheri*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出"因果掩蔽问题"，指出基于平均处理效应（ATE）的公平性监管存在缺陷，即使ATE为零，优化算法仍可通过因果掩蔽导致显著不平等，强调需要在模型层面而非决策层面进行公平性监管。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于因果理论的公平性量化框架主要关注平均处理效应（ATE），但作者认为基于平均值的监管存在问题。他们关注到即使ATE为零，仍可能存在显著的不平等处理，需要揭示这种监管漏洞。

Method: 将"因果掩蔽问题"表述为线性规划问题，在保持保护属性与决策之间ATE为零的条件下，优化替代目标（如最大化利润或最小化犯罪）。通过研究因果掩蔽的能力和局限性，分析优化算法如何导致不平等。

Result: 研究表明，基于ATE的监管优化可能引发显著的不平等处理。真实公平性与因果掩蔽公平性之间的差异主要由混淆因素驱动，强调了在评估公平性时进行完全条件独立性测试的重要性。因果掩蔽解决方案在统计和信息论上难以检测，可能长期存在。

Conclusion: 必须在模型层面而非决策层面进行公平性监管，因为基于ATE的监管存在根本性缺陷，无法有效防止算法通过因果掩蔽实现不平等。

Abstract: Recent work has proposed powerful frameworks, rooted in causal theory, to quantify fairness. Causal inference has primarily emphasized the detection of \emph{average} treatment effects (ATEs), and subsequent notions of fairness have inherited this focus. In this paper, we build on previous concerns about regulation based on averages. In particular, we formulate the "causal masking problem" as a linear program that optimizes an alternative objective, such as maximizing profit or minimizing crime, while retaining a zero ATE (i.e., the ATE between a protected attribute and a decision). By studying the capabilities and limitations of causal masking, we show that optimization under ATE-based regulation may induce significant unequal treatment. We demonstrate that the divergence between true and causally masked fairness is driven by confounding, underscoring the importance of full conditional-independence testing when assessing fairness. Finally, we discuss statistical and information-theoretic limitations that make causally masked solutions very difficult to detect, allowing them to persist for long periods. These results argue that we must regulate fairness at the model-level, rather than at the decision level.

</details>


### [6] [Deep Generative Spatiotemporal Engression for Probabilistic Forecasting of Epidemics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07108)
*Rajdeep Pathak,Tanujit Chakraborty*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文提出了深度时空回归方法，用于生成低频流行病数据集的准确可靠概率预测，通过轻量级深度生成架构量化不确定性，在多个流行病数据集上优于现有基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流行病发病率预测对公共卫生准备至关重要，但由于复杂的非线性时间依赖性和异质性空间交互，这仍然是一个具有挑战性的任务。传统的点预测在不确定性分配方面不可靠，而概率预测能够提供最佳或最坏情况场景，比简单的点估计更有价值。

Method: 提出了深度时空回归方法，作为分布透镜，通过从训练模型中采样生成样本外概率预测。框架包含轻量级深度生成架构，其中不确定性通过模型构建期间的预加噪声组件内生量化。建立了在温和假设下时空回归过程的几何遍历性和渐近平稳性。

Result: 在三个预测时间范围内对六个流行病数据集进行全面评估表明，该方法在点预测和概率预测方面都持续优于多个时间和时空基准方法。同时探索了提案的可解释性，以增强模型在知情、及时公共卫生干预中的实际应用。

Conclusion: 深度时空回归方法能够生成准确可靠的流行病概率预测，通过轻量级架构内生量化不确定性，在多个数据集上表现优于现有方法，并具有实际公共卫生应用潜力。

Abstract: Accurate and reliable forecasting of epidemic incidences is critical for public health preparedness, yet it remains a challenging task due to complex nonlinear temporal dependencies and heterogeneous spatial interactions. Often, point forecasts generated by spatiotemporal models are unreliable in assigning uncertainty to future epidemic events. Probabilistic forecasting of epidemics is therefore crucial for providing the best or worst-case scenarios rather than a simple, often inaccurate, point estimate. We present deep spatiotemporal engression methods to generate accurate and reliable probabilistic forecasts on low-frequency epidemic datasets. The proposed methods act as distributional lenses, and out-of-sample probabilistic forecasts are generated by sampling from the trained models. Our frameworks encapsulate lightweight deep generative architectures, wherein uncertainty is quantified endogenously, driven by a pre-additive noise component during model construction. We establish geometric ergodicity and asymptotic stationarity of the spatiotemporal engression processes under mild assumptions on the network weights and pre-additive noise process. Comprehensive evaluations across six epidemiological datasets over three forecast horizons demonstrate that the proposal consistently outperforms several temporal and spatiotemporal benchmarks in both point and probabilistic forecasting. Additionally, we explore the explainability of the proposal to enhance the models' practical application for informed, timely public health interventions.

</details>


### [7] [Probabilistic Inference and Learning with Stein's Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07467)
*Qiang Liu,Lester Mackey,Chris Oates*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该专著系统阐述了基于Stein方法的概率推断与学习的理论与方法，包括Stein差异的构建、性质分析及其与Stein变分梯度下降的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为概率推断和学习领域提供一个严谨的理论框架，系统化Stein方法的应用，填补该领域系统化理论指导的空白。

Method: 从Stein算子和Stein集合构建Stein差异，分析其可计算性、分离性、收敛检测和控制等性质，并详细阐述Stein算子与Stein变分梯度下降的关联。

Result: 提供了完整的理论框架和方法论体系，包括精确的主要定义和结果陈述，以及所有证明的参考文献。

Conclusion: 该专著为基于Stein方法的概率推断和学习建立了系统化的理论基础，为相关研究和应用提供了全面的理论指导和方法论支持。

Abstract: This monograph provides a rigorous overview of theoretical and methodological aspects of probabilistic inference and learning with Stein's method. Recipes are provided for constructing Stein discrepancies from Stein operators and Stein sets, and properties of these discrepancies such as computability, separation, convergence detection, and convergence control are discussed. Further, the connection between Stein operators and Stein variational gradient descent is set out in detail. The main definitions and results are precisely stated, and references to all proofs are provided.

</details>


### [8] [Beyond Data Splitting: Full-Data Conformal Prediction by Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07522)
*Young Hyun Cho,Jordan Awan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需数据分割的隐私保护共形预测框架，通过差分隐私的稳定性控制样本内外分数差距，结合保守的私有分位数算法防止覆盖率不足


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私保护和不确定性量化在数据驱动决策中日益重要。现有隐私共形预测方法通常依赖数据分割，这会减少有效样本量，因此需要开发无需分割的全数据隐私保护方法

Method: 提出全数据隐私保护共形预测框架：1) 利用差分隐私诱导的稳定性控制样本内外共形分数的差距；2) 结合保守的私有分位数算法防止覆盖率不足；3) 提供机制特定的稳定性分析以实现名义覆盖率的渐近恢复

Result: 实验表明该方法比基于分割的隐私基线产生更精确的预测集。通用差分隐私保证产生统一的覆盖率下限，但通常无法恢复名义1-α水平；而机制特定的稳定性分析能够实现名义水平的渐近恢复

Conclusion: 该框架避免了数据分割，利用差分隐私的稳定性特性，通过机制特定的分析实现了名义覆盖率的渐近恢复，在隐私保护的同时提供了更精确的预测集

Abstract: Privacy protection and uncertainty quantification are increasingly important in data-driven decision making. Conformal prediction provides finite-sample marginal coverage, but existing private approaches often rely on data splitting, reducing the effective sample size. We propose a full-data privacy-preserving conformal prediction framework that avoids splitting. Our framework leverages stability induced by differential privacy to control the gap between in-sample and out-of-sample conformal scores, and pairs this with a conservative private quantile routine designed to prevent under-coverage. We show that a generic differential privacy guarantee yields a universal coverage floor, yet cannot generally recover the nominal $1-α$ level. We then provide a refined, mechanism-specific stability analysis and yields asymptotic recovery of the nominal level. Experiments demonstrate sharper prediction sets than the split-based private baseline.

</details>


### [9] [An Interpretable Generative Framework for Anomaly Detection in High-Dimensional Financial Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07864)
*Waldyn G Martinez*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: ReGEN-TAD是一个可解释的生成框架，结合机器学习和计量经济学诊断，用于检测高维金融时间序列中的结构不稳定性和异常


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维金融时间序列中的结构不稳定性和异常检测面临挑战，因为存在复杂的时间依赖性和演变的横截面结构

Method: 提出ReGEN-TAD框架，结合联合预测和重构，采用改进的卷积-Transformer架构，聚合预测不一致性、重构退化、潜在失真和波动率变化等互补信号，通过稳健校准生成统一的异常分数

Result: 在合成和金融面板数据上的实验表明，该方法对结构化偏差具有改进的鲁棒性，同时能够实现经济上一致的因素级归因

Conclusion: ReGEN-TAD是一个有效的可解释生成框架，能够检测高维金融时间序列中的异常，无需标记数据，具有经济解释性

Abstract: Detecting structural instability and anomalies in high-dimensional financial time series is challenging due to complex temporal dependence and evolving cross-sectional structure. We propose ReGEN-TAD, an interpretable generative framework that integrates modern machine learning with econometric diagnostics for anomaly detection. The model combines joint forecasting and reconstruction within a refined convolutional--transformer architecture and aggregates complementary signals capturing predictive inconsistency, reconstruction degradation, latent distortion, and volatility shifts. Robust calibration yields a unified anomaly score without labeled data. Experiments on synthetic and financial panels demonstrate improved robustness to structured deviations while enabling economically coherent factor-level attribution.

</details>


### [10] [Robust Transfer Learning with Side Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07921)
*Akram S. Awad,Shihab Ahmed,Yue Wang,George K. Atia*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种基于估计中心不确定性集的鲁棒MDP框架，利用源-目标域动态的侧信息（特征矩、分布距离、密度比）构建更紧的不确定性集，在环境转移下获得更好的目标域策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分布鲁棒优化方法在面对大的环境转移时需要扩大不确定性集，导致策略过于保守和悲观。需要一种能够利用有限目标样本和源-目标动态侧信息的方法来构建更精确的不确定性集。

Method: 通过约束估计方法构建估计中心的不确定性集，整合有限的目标样本和源-目标动态的侧信息（包括特征矩边界、分布距离、密度比），获得改进的转移核估计和更紧的不确定性集。

Result: 建立了鲁棒和非鲁棒值函数的误差界和收敛结果，提供了学习鲁棒策略的有限样本保证，分析了鲁棒次优性间隙。在转移模型具有低维结构时，侧信息能减少次优性间隙并提高样本效率。

Conclusion: 在OpenAI Gym环境和经典控制问题上的实验表明，该方法在目标域性能上优于当前最先进的鲁棒和非鲁棒基线方法，证明了利用侧信息构建更紧不确定性集的有效性。

Abstract: Robust Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) address environmental shift through distributionally robust optimization (DRO) by finding an optimal worst-case policy within an uncertainty set of transition kernels. However, standard DRO approaches require enlarging the uncertainty set under large shifts, which leads to overly conservative and pessimistic policies.
  In this paper, we propose a framework for transfer under environment shift that derives a robust target-domain policy via estimate-centered uncertainty sets, constructed through constrained estimation that integrates limited target samples with side information about the source-target dynamics. The side information includes bounds on feature moments, distributional distances, and density ratios, yielding improved kernel estimates and tighter uncertainty sets.
  The side information includes bounds on feature moments, distributional distances, and density ratios, yielding improved kernel estimates and tighter uncertainty sets.
  Error bounds and convergence results are established for both robust and non-robust value functions. Moreover, we provide a finite-sample guarantee on the learned robust policy and analyze the robust sub-optimality gap. Under mild low-dimensional structure on the transition model, the side information reduces this gap and improves sample efficiency. We assess the performance of our approach across OpenAI Gym environments and classic control problems, consistently demonstrating superior target-domain performance over state-of-the-art robust and non-robust baselines.

</details>


### [11] [Beyond ReinMax: Low-Variance Gradient Estimators for Discrete Latent Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08257)
*Daniel Wang,Thang D. Bui*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了ReinMax-Rao和ReinMax-CV两种新的梯度估计器，通过结合Rao-Blackwell化和控制变量技术来降低ReinMax估计器的方差，在离散隐变量变分自编码器训练中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ReinMax梯度估计器虽然通过Heun方法减少了偏差，但存在高方差的问题，这限制了其在离散隐变量机器学习模型中的应用效果。

Method: 提出了两种改进的梯度估计器：1) ReinMax-Rao：结合Rao-Blackwell化技术降低方差；2) ReinMax-CV：使用控制变量技术减少方差。同时从数值积分角度提供了ReinMax的替代视角。

Result: 新提出的ReinMax-Rao和ReinMax-CV估计器在训练离散隐变量变分自编码器时表现出优于现有方法的性能，有效降低了梯度估计的方差。

Conclusion: 通过结合方差减少技术，成功改进了ReinMax梯度估计器，为离散隐变量模型的训练提供了更有效的梯度估计方法，并为进一步利用数值方法构建更精确的梯度近似提供了研究思路。

Abstract: Machine learning models involving discrete latent variables require gradient estimators to facilitate backpropagation in a computationally efficient manner. The most recent addition to the Straight-Through family of estimators, ReinMax, can be viewed from a numerical ODE perspective as incorporating an approximation via Heun's method to reduce bias, but at the cost of high variance. In this work, we introduce the ReinMax-Rao and ReinMax-CV estimators which incorporate Rao-Blackwellisation and control variate techniques into ReinMax to reduce its variance. Our estimators demonstrate superior performance on training variational autoencoders with discrete latent spaces. Furthermore, we investigate the possibility of leveraging alternative numerical methods for constructing more accurate gradient approximations and present an alternative view of ReinMax from a simpler numerical integration perspective.

</details>


### [12] [Posterior Sampling Reinforcement Learning with Gaussian Processes for Continuous Control: Sublinear Regret Bounds for Unbounded State Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08287)
*Hamish Flynn,Joe Watson,Ingmar Posner,Jan Peters*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文分析了高斯过程后验采样强化学习（GP-PSRL）算法的贝叶斯遗憾，通过递归应用Borell-Tsirelson-Ibragimov-Sudakov不等式和控制链方法，获得了紧致的遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后验采样是处理不确定性决策的有效启发式方法，已成功应用于各种连续控制问题。然而，GP-PSRL的理论研究有限，现有遗憾界要么未能达到对最大信息增益的紧致依赖，要么未能正确处理状态空间无界的问题。

Method: 通过递归应用Borell-Tsirelson-Ibragimov-Sudakov不等式，证明算法实际访问的状态以高概率包含在近常数半径的球内。使用链式方法控制GP-PSRL的遗憾，获得紧致的最大信息增益依赖。

Result: 获得了贝叶斯遗憾界为$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(H^{3/2}\sqrt{γ_{T/H} T})$，其中$H$是时间步长，$T$是时间步数，$γ_{T/H}$是最大信息增益。这一结果解决了先前PSRL理论工作的局限性。

Conclusion: 该研究为分析复杂设置中的PSRL提供了理论基础和工具，解决了先前理论工作的限制，获得了紧致的遗憾界，为后验采样强化学习的理论分析做出了重要贡献。

Abstract: We analyze the Bayesian regret of the Gaussian process posterior sampling reinforcement learning (GP-PSRL) algorithm. Posterior sampling is an effective heuristic for decision-making under uncertainty that has been used to develop successful algorithms for a variety of continuous control problems. However, theoretical work on GP-PSRL is limited. All known regret bounds either fail to achieve a tight dependence on a kernel-dependent quantity called the maximum information gain or fail to properly account for the fact that the set of possible system states is unbounded. Through a recursive application of the Borell-Tsirelson-Ibragimov-Sudakov inequality, we show that, with high probability, the states actually visited by the algorithm are contained within a ball of near-constant radius. To obtain tight dependence on the maximum information gain, we use the chaining method to control the regret suffered by GP-PSRL. Our main result is a Bayesian regret bound of the order $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(H^{3/2}\sqrt{γ_{T/H} T})$, where $H$ is the horizon, $T$ is the number of time steps and $γ_{T/H}$ is the maximum information gain. With this result, we resolve the limitations with prior theoretical work on PSRL, and provide the theoretical foundation and tools for analyzing PSRL in complex settings.

</details>


### [13] [Unifying On- and Off-Policy Variance Reduction Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08370)
*Olivier Jeunen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文建立了在线A/B测试与离策略评估之间的正式等价关系，证明标准在线差异均值估计器与配备最优控制变量的逆倾向评分估计器在数学上相同，回归调整方法（如CUPED、CUPAC、ML-RATE）与双重稳健估计在结构上等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线A/B测试和离策略评估虽然共享估计处理增量值的共同目标，但在实践中往往孤立运作，使用不同的术语和统计工具。这种分离限制了知识交流和方法的相互借鉴。

Method: 通过建立形式等价关系，证明标准在线差异均值估计器与配备最优（方差最小化）加性控制变量的逆倾向评分估计器在数学上相同。进一步证明回归调整方法与双重稳健估计的结构等价性。

Result: 建立了在线A/B测试与离策略评估之间的正式等价关系，揭示了两种领域中常用方差减少方法的数学统一性，为实践者和研究者提供了跨领域的理解框架。

Conclusion: 这种统一视角扩展了对常用方法的理解，能够指导从事这两类问题的实践者和研究人员，促进跨领域知识交流和方法创新。

Abstract: Continuous and efficient experimentation is key to the practical success of user-facing applications on the web, both through online A/B-tests and off-policy evaluation. Despite their shared objective -- estimating the incremental value of a treatment -- these domains often operate in isolation, utilising distinct terminologies and statistical toolkits. This paper bridges that divide by establishing a formal equivalence between their canonical variance reduction methods.
  We prove that the standard online Difference-in-Means estimator is mathematically identical to an off-policy Inverse Propensity Scoring estimator equipped with an optimal (variance-minimising) additive control variate. Extending this unification, we demonstrate that widespread regression adjustment methods (such as CUPED, CUPAC, and ML-RATE) are structurally equivalent to Doubly Robust estimation. This unified view extends our understanding of commonly used approaches, and can guide practitioners and researchers working on either class of problems.

</details>


### [14] [Generative Adversarial Regression (GAR): Learning Conditional Risk Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08553)
*Saeed Asadi,Jonathan Yu-Meng Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出生成对抗回归(GAR)框架，通过生成器学习条件风险场景，使生成场景与下游风险目标对齐，特别适用于可引出函数如分位数、期望分位数等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法在生成风险场景时难以确保与下游风险目标对齐，特别是在条件风险建模中。需要一种能够生成与特定风险函数（如VaR、ES）对齐的场景的方法，以支持更准确的风险评估和决策。

Method: 基于可引出函数的回归特征，将点预测扩展到生成建模。采用极小极大化公式，训练生成器使其策略诱导的风险与真实数据在相同上下文下的风险匹配。对抗性策略识别最坏情况下的风险评估差异，生成器则适应消除这些差异。

Result: 在S&P 500数据上的实验表明，GAR生成的场景比无条件、计量经济学和直接预测基线方法更好地保持下游风险，同时在对抗性选择的策略下保持稳定。特别展示了基于联合可引出(VaR, ES)目标的尾部风险实例化效果。

Conclusion: GAR框架提供了一种有效的方法来学习与下游风险目标对齐的条件风险场景，通过对抗性训练确保在广泛策略类别中的鲁棒性，为风险管理和金融建模提供了新的工具。

Abstract: We propose Generative Adversarial Regression (GAR), a framework for learning conditional risk scenarios through generators aligned with downstream risk objectives. GAR builds on a regression characterization of conditional risk for elicitable functionals, including quantiles, expectiles, and jointly elicitable pairs. We extend this principle from point prediction to generative modeling by training generators whose policy-induced risk matches that of real data under the same context. To ensure robustness across all policies, GAR adopts a minimax formulation in which an adversarial policy identifies worst-case discrepancies in risk evaluation while the generator adapts to eliminate them. This structure preserves alignment with the risk functional across a broad class of policies rather than a fixed, pre-specified set. We illustrate GAR through a tail-risk instantiation based on jointly elicitable $(\mathrm{VaR}, \mathrm{ES})$ objectives. Experiments on S\&P 500 data show that GAR produces scenarios that better preserve downstream risk than unconditional, econometric, and direct predictive baselines while remaining stable under adversarially selected policies.

</details>


### [15] [Momentum SVGD-EM for Accelerated Maximum Marginal Likelihood Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08676)
*Adam Rozzio,Rafael Athanasiades,O. Deniz Akyildiz*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Nesterov加速的Momentum SVGD-EM方法，通过在参数更新和概率测度空间中引入动量加速，显著提升了最大边际似然估计的收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然已有工作从自由能泛函优化的角度将EM算法解释为坐标下降法，并基于此开发了交互粒子算法，但这些方法在收敛速度上仍有改进空间。本文旨在通过引入Nesterov加速技术来提升基于Stein变分梯度下降的MMLE算法的收敛效率。

Method: 提出Momentum SVGD-EM方法，在参数更新和概率测度空间中同时引入Nesterov动量加速。该方法基于Stein变分梯度下降框架，通过动量项来加速收敛过程，适用于低维和高维设置。

Result: Momentum SVGD-EM在各种难度递增的任务中都能一致地加速收敛，显著减少了所需的迭代次数。该方法在低维和高维设置中都表现出有效性，证明了加速策略的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过在SVGD-EM框架中引入Nesterov动量加速，成功开发了一种收敛更快的最大边际似然估计算法。该方法为基于自由能泛函优化的MMLE问题提供了有效的加速解决方案。

Abstract: Maximum marginal likelihood estimation (MMLE) can be formulated as the optimization of a free energy functional. From this viewpoint, the Expectation-Maximisation (EM) algorithm admits a natural interpretation as a coordinate descent method over the joint space of model parameters and probability measures. Recently, a significant body of work has adopted this perspective, leading to interacting particle algorithms for MMLE. In this paper, we propose an accelerated version of one such procedure, based on Stein variational gradient descent (SVGD), by introducing Nesterov acceleration in both the parameter updates and in the space of probability measures. The resulting method, termed Momentum SVGD-EM, consistently accelerates convergence in terms of required iterations across various tasks of increasing difficulty, demonstrating effectiveness in both low- and high-dimensional settings.

</details>


### [16] [Structural Causal Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08682)
*Simon Bing,Jonas Wahl,Jakob Runge*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出结构因果瓶颈模型（SCBMs），假设高维变量间的因果效应仅依赖于原因的低维摘要统计量（瓶颈），为任务特定降维提供灵活框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果降维框架如因果表示学习或因果抽象学习存在局限性，需要一种能够捕捉因果效应本质的降维方法，同时保持可估计性和实用性

Method: 引入结构因果瓶颈模型（SCBMs），假设因果效应仅依赖于原因的低维瓶颈统计量，可通过标准简单学习算法进行估计，分析可识别性，并与信息瓶颈理论建立联系

Result: SCBMs提供灵活的任务特定降维框架，在低样本迁移学习环境中展示瓶颈对效应估计的益处，实验验证了模型的可行性

Conclusion: SCBMs为现有因果降维框架提供了有前景的替代方案，通过瓶颈机制实现高效因果效应估计，特别适用于低样本迁移学习场景

Abstract: We introduce structural causal bottleneck models (SCBMs), a novel class of structural causal models. At the core of SCBMs lies the assumption that causal effects between high-dimensional variables only depend on low-dimensional summary statistics, or bottlenecks, of the causes. SCBMs provide a flexible framework for task-specific dimension reduction while being estimable via standard, simple learning algorithms in practice. We analyse identifiability in SCBMs, connect them to information bottlenecks in the sense of Tishby & Zaslavsky (2015), and illustrate how to estimate them experimentally. We also demonstrate the benefit of bottlenecks for effect estimation in low-sample transfer learning settings. We argue that SCBMs provide an alternative to existing causal dimension reduction frameworks like causal representation learning or causal abstraction learning.

</details>

### [17] [Scaling Strategy, Not Compute: A Stand-Alone, Open-Source StarCraft II Benchmark for Accessible Reinforcement Learning Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06608)
*Sourav Panda,Shreyash Kale,Tanmay Ambadkar,Abhinav Verma,Jonathan Dodge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Two-Bridge Map Suite填补了星际争霸II完整游戏与迷你游戏之间的复杂度空白，作为中间基准测试环境，专注于战术技能学习


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究社区缺乏星际争霸II完整游戏与迷你游戏之间的中间复杂度环境。完整游戏状态-动作空间庞大导致奖励信号稀疏嘈杂，而迷你游戏中简单智能体就能达到饱和性能。这种复杂度差距阻碍了渐进式课程设计，使研究人员难以在现实计算预算下在RTS环境中实验现代强化学习算法

Method: 通过禁用经济机制（资源收集、基地建设、战争迷雾），创建Two-Bridge Map Suite作为中间基准测试环境。该环境专注于两个核心战术技能：长距离导航和微操作战斗。作为轻量级、兼容Gym的PySC2包装器发布，包含地图、包装器和参考脚本

Result: 初步实验显示，智能体能够学习连贯的机动和交战行为，而无需承担完整游戏的计算成本。该环境成功填补了复杂度空白

Conclusion: Two-Bridge Map Suite作为开源基准测试系列的首个条目，为强化学习研究提供了星际争霸II完整游戏与迷你游戏之间的中间复杂度环境，促进了在现实计算预算下的RTS算法实验

Abstract: The research community lacks a middle ground between StarCraft IIs full game and its mini-games. The full-games sprawling state-action space renders reward signals sparse and noisy, but in mini-games simple agents saturate performance. This complexity gap hinders steady curriculum design and prevents researchers from experimenting with modern Reinforcement Learning algorithms in RTS environments under realistic compute budgets. To fill this gap, we present the Two-Bridge Map Suite, the first entry in an open-source benchmark series we purposely engineered as an intermediate benchmark to sit between these extremes. By disabling economy mechanics such as resource collection, base building, and fog-of-war, the environment isolates two core tactical skills: long-range navigation and micro-combat. Preliminary experiments show that agents learn coherent maneuvering and engagement behaviors without imposing full-game computational costs. Two-Bridge is released as a lightweight, Gym-compatible wrapper on top of PySC2, with maps, wrappers, and reference scripts fully open-sourced to encourage broad adoption as a standard benchmark.

</details>


### [18] [MultiGen: Level-Design for Editable Multiplayer Worlds in Diffusion Game Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06679)
*Ryan Po,David Junhao Zhang,Amir Hertz,Gordon Wetzstein,Neal Wadhwa,Nataniel Ruiz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种具有显式外部记忆的视频世界模型，通过可编辑的记忆表示实现用户对环境的直接控制，并支持实时多人协同推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视频世界模型在交互性方面存在两个主要限制：1）用户难以对环境进行可重现、可编辑的控制；2）缺乏支持玩家共同影响同一世界的共享推理机制。

Method: 引入显式外部记忆作为系统的持久状态，独立于模型的上下文窗口，通过用户动作持续更新并在生成过程中查询。将生成过程分解为记忆、观察和动态三个模块，取代传统的下一帧预测器方法。

Result: 该方法实现了用户通过可编辑记忆表示直接控制环境结构，并自然扩展到具有一致视角和跨玩家交互的实时多人协同生成。

Conclusion: 通过显式外部记忆和模块化设计，该研究解决了视频世界模型在用户控制和共享推理方面的关键限制，为交互式模拟和娱乐应用提供了更强大的框架。

Abstract: Video world models have shown immense promise for interactive simulation and entertainment, but current systems still struggle with two important aspects of interactivity: user control over the environment for reproducible, editable experiences, and shared inference where players hold influence over a common world. To address these limitations, we introduce an explicit external memory into the system, a persistent state operating independent of the model's context window, that is continually updated by user actions and queried throughout the generation roll-out. Unlike conventional diffusion game engines that operate as next-frame predictors, our approach decomposes generation into Memory, Observation, and Dynamics modules. This design gives users direct, editable control over environment structure via an editable memory representation, and it naturally extends to real-time multiplayer rollouts with coherent viewpoints and consistent cross-player interactions.

</details>


### [19] [Best-of-Tails: Bridging Optimism and Pessimism in Inference-Time Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06797)
*Hsiang Hsu,Eric Lei,Chun-Fu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Best-of-Tails (BoT) 是一个自适应推理时对齐框架，通过分析奖励分布的尾部特性，动态调整选择策略来平衡探索收益与对齐误差，优于固定策略基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前推理时对齐方法面临基本困境：乐观方法（如Best-of-N）容易受到奖励攻击，而悲观的正则化方法则抑制了发现高质量响应所需的探索。需要一种能够根据奖励分布特性自适应调整的策略。

Method: 提出Best-of-Tails (BoT)框架，使用Tsallis散度作为可调正则化器，在乐观和悲观极端之间提供更精细的插值。通过Hill估计器对每个提示的奖励尾部特性进行表征，并动态调整选择规则来平衡探索收益与对齐误差。

Result: 在数学、多项选择推理和人类偏好评估中，BoT相对于固定策略基线，在各种参考模型和奖励模型配置下都提高了对齐性能。

Conclusion: 最优推理时对齐策略关键取决于奖励分布的尾部行为：轻尾机制有利于乐观策略以发现高质量异常值，而重尾机制需要悲观策略以防止极端情况下的奖励校准错误。BoT通过自适应调整成功解决了这一权衡问题。

Abstract: Inference-time alignment effectively steers large language models (LLMs) by generating multiple candidates from a reference model and selecting among them with an imperfect reward model. However, current strategies face a fundamental dilemma: ``optimistic'' approaches like Best-of-$N$ suffer from reward hacking, while ``pessimistic'' regularized methods often stifle the exploration needed to discover high-quality responses. In this work, we formalize this trade-off through the lens of regret minimization, demonstrating that the optimal strategy depends critically on the tail behavior of the reward distribution. We show theoretically that light-tailed regimes favor optimism to unearth high-quality outliers, whereas heavy-tailed regimes require pessimism to guard against reward mis-calibration in the extremes. Guided by this insight, we introduce Best-of-Tails (BoT), an adaptive inference-time alignment framework that uses Tsallis divergence as a tunable regularizer to provide a finer granularity of interpolation between these extremes. BoT uses the Hill estimator to characterize reward-tail heaviness on a per-prompt basis and dynamically adjusts its selection rule to balance exploration gains against alignment error. Across math, multiple-choice reasoning, and human-preference evaluations, BoT improves alignment performance across a range of reference and reward model configurations relative to fixed-strategy baselines.

</details>


### [20] [Breaking the Martingale Curse: Multi-Agent Debate via Asymmetric Cognitive Potential Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06801)
*Yuhan Liu,Juntian Zhang,Yichen Wu,Martin Takac,Salem Lahlou,Xiuying Chen,Nils Lukas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AceMAD框架通过利用非对称认知势能打破多智能体辩论中的鞅诅咒，将随机游走转变为具有正向漂移的定向收敛过程


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准多智能体辩论存在鞅诅咒问题：相关误差导致智能体收敛于错误共识，辩论只是强化集体错误而非过滤噪声，无法超越多数投票的正确性

Method: 提出AceMAD框架，通过同伴预测机制揭示非对称认知势能：真理持有者不仅知道正确答案，还能预测群体的误解，而幻觉多数则无法察觉集体错误。使用严格适当评分规则量化这种势能差距，通过非线性聚合将认知优势转化为向真理收敛的亚鞅漂移

Result: 在六个基准测试的挑战性子集上，AceMAD即使在初始多数错误的情况下也能恢复稀疏的真理信号，显著优于基线方法

Conclusion: AceMAD通过利用非对称认知势能打破了多智能体辩论中的鞅诅咒，将辩论从随机游走转变为具有正向漂移的定向收敛过程，有效提升了语言模型的推理能力

Abstract: Multi-Agent Debate (MAD) has emerged as a promising paradigm for enhancing large language model reasoning. However, recent work reveals a limitation:standard MAD cannot improve belief correctness beyond majority voting; we refer to this as the Martingale Curse. This curse arises because correlated errors cause agents to converge toward erroneous consensus, where debate merely reinforces collective mistakes rather than filtering noise. We propose AceMAD, a framework that breaks the Martingale Curse by harnessing asymmetric cognitive potential energy to transform MAD from a random walk into a directed convergence process with positive drift. Through a peer-prediction mechanism, agents predict their peers' belief distributions, revealing asymmetric cognitive potential: truth-holders not only know the correct answer but also anticipate the crowd's misconceptions, while the hallucinating majority remains blind to their collective error. This asymmetry creates a potential energy gap that we quantify via strictly proper scoring rules. We prove this cognitive potential manifests as information-theoretic superiority and, under nonlinear aggregation, converts into submartingale drift toward truth, directly breaking the Martingale Curse. Experiments on challenging subsets across six benchmarks show AceMAD recovers sparse truth signals even when initial majorities are incorrect, substantially outperforming baseline methods.

</details>


### [21] [Making AI Evaluation Deployment Relevant Through Context Specification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06811)
*Matthew Holmes,Thiago Lacerda,Reva Schwartz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"上下文规范"作为AI评估新方法，将模糊的利益相关者观点转化为明确的可测量构念，帮助组织更准确地评估AI部署价值


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多组织难以从AI部署中获得价值，当前AI评估方法掩盖了决定部署成功的操作现实，使决策者难以判断AI工具是否能提供持久价值

Method: 引入"上下文规范"过程，将利益相关者关于特定环境中重要因素的分散观点转化为清晰、命名的构念，明确定义评估旨在捕获的属性、行为和结果，使其能够在上下文中被观察和测量

Result: 上下文规范过程为评估AI系统在组织实际管理的部署环境中可能表现提供了基础路线图

Conclusion: 上下文规范是支持AI部署决策的关键过程，通过将模糊的期望转化为可测量的构念，帮助组织更有效地评估AI系统的实际价值和部署可行性

Abstract: With many organizations struggling to gain value from AI deployments, pressure to evaluate AI in an informed manner has intensified. Status quo AI evaluation approaches mask the operational realities that ultimately determine deployment success, making it difficult for decision makers outside the stack to know whether and how AI tools will deliver durable value. We introduce and describe context specification as a process to support and inform the deployment decision making process. Context specification turns diffuse stakeholder perspectives about what matters in a given setting into clear, named constructs: explicit definitions of the properties, behaviors, and outcomes that evaluations aim to capture, so they can be observed and measured in context. The process serves as a foundational roadmap for evaluating what AI systems are likely to do in the deployment contexts that organizations actually manage.

</details>


### [22] [Reinforcing the World's Edge: A Continual Learning Problem in the Multi-Agent-World Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06813)
*Dane Malenfant*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文探讨了强化学习中可重用决策结构在不同情境下的稳定性，特别是当智能体-世界边界发生变化时，这种结构如何受到影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是理解在强化学习中，跨情节可重用的决策结构如何受到智能体-世界边界定义的影响，特别是在多智能体环境中，当同伴智能体被纳入"世界"部分时，这种结构可能变得不稳定。

Method: 方法包括：1）在平稳有限时域MDP中构建不变核心（所有成功轨迹共享的状态-动作对子序列）；2）在目标条件假设下证明其存在性；3）将相同任务嵌入去中心化马尔可夫博弈中，将同伴智能体纳入世界部分；4）量化策略诱导的非平稳性，使用诱导核和奖励的变分预算。

Result: 结果显示：1）在平稳MDP中，不变核心可以构建并证明存在；2）在去中心化马尔可夫博弈中，每个同伴策略更新都会诱导新的MDP，导致每情节不变核心可能缩小或消失；3）即使诱导世界动态发生微小变化，也可能导致核心消失，只留下个体任务核心或完全消失。

Conclusion: 结论认为，持续强化学习问题源于去中心化多智能体强化学习中智能体-世界边界的不稳定性，而非外生任务切换。这为未来研究如何保持、预测或管理边界漂移提供了方向。

Abstract: Reusable decision structure survives across episodes in reinforcement learning, but this depends on how the agent--world boundary is drawn. In stationary, finite-horizon MDPs, an invariant core: the (not-necessarily contiguous) subsequences of state--action pairs shared by all successful trajectories (optionally under a simple abstraction) can be constructed. Under mild goal-conditioned assumptions, it's existence can be proven and explained by how the core captures prototypes that transfer across episodes. When the same task is embedded in a decentralized Markov game and the peer agent is folded into the world, each peer-policy update induces a new MDP; the per-episode invariant core can shrink or vanish, even with small changes to the induced world dynamics, sometimes leaving only the individual task core or just nothing. This policy-induced non-stationarity can be quantified with a variation budget over the induced kernels and rewards, linking boundary drift to loss of invariants. The view that a continual RL problem arises from instability of the agent--world boundary (rather than exogenous task switches) in decentralized MARL suggests future work on preserving, predicting, or otherwise managing boundary drift.

</details>


### [23] [Symmetry-Constrained Language-Guided Program Synthesis for Discovering Governing Equations from Noisy and Partial Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06869)
*Mirza Samad Ahmed Baig,Syeda Anshrah Gillani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SymLang是一个统一框架，通过对称性约束语法、语言模型引导的程序合成和贝叶斯模型选择，从噪声观测数据中发现物理方程，在133个动力系统中实现83.7%的结构恢复率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从实验观测中发现紧凑的控制方程是定量科学的核心目标，但现有方法在数据噪声、未观测变量或符号结构多解等情况下经常失败，需要更鲁棒的发现框架。

Method: 结合三个创新：1) 类型化对称性约束语法，编码量纲分析、群论不变性和奇偶约束；2) 语言模型引导的程序合成，使用微调的7B参数模型；3) MDL正则化的贝叶斯模型选择与块自举稳定性分析。

Result: 在133个动力系统测试中，在10%观测噪声下实现83.7%的精确结构恢复率，比最佳基线提高22.4个百分点，外推误差减少61%，物理漂移降低至3.1×10⁻³（竞争对手为187.3×10⁻³）。

Conclusion: SymLang提供了一个从原始数据到可解释、物理可审计符号定律的原则性路径，能正确识别结构简并性并报告不确定性，而非返回错误单一方程。

Abstract: Discovering compact governing equations from experimental observations is one of the defining objectives of quantitative science, yet practical discovery pipelines routinely fail when measurements are noisy, relevant state variables are unobserved, or multiple symbolic structures explain the data equally well within statistical uncertainty. Here we introduce SymLang (Symmetry-constrained Language-guided equation discovery), a unified framework that brings together three previously separate ideas: (i) typed symmetry-constrained grammars that encode dimensional analysis, group-theoretic invariance, and parity constraints as hard production rules, eliminating on average 71.3% of candidate expression trees before any fitting; (ii) language-model-guided program synthesis in which a fine-tuned 7B-parameter proposer, conditioned on interpretable data descriptors, efficiently navigates the constrained search space; and (iii) MDL-regularized Bayesian model selection coupled with block-bootstrap stability analysis that quantifies structural uncertainty rather than committing to a single best equation. Across 133 dynamical systems spanning classical mechanics, electrodynamics, thermodynamics, population dynamics, and nonlinear oscillators, SymLang achieves an exact structural recovery rate of 83.7% under 10% observational noise - a 22.4 percentage-point improvement over the next-best baseline - while reducing out-of-distribution extrapolation error by 61% and near-eliminating conservation-law violations (3.1 x 10-3 vs. 187.3 x 10-3 physical drift for the closest competitor). In all tested regimes the framework correctly identifies structural degeneracy, reporting it explicitly rather than returning a confidently wrong single equation. The framework is fully open-source and reproducible, providing a principled pathway from raw data to interpretable, physically auditable symbolic laws.

</details>


### [24] [LEAD: Breaking the No-Recovery Bottleneck in Long-Horizon Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06870)
*Denys Pushkin,Emmanuel Abbe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出LEAD方法解决LLM长程执行中的"无恢复瓶颈"问题，通过前瞻验证和重叠rollout聚合，在保持稳定性的同时提供足够上下文纠正错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在长程执行中即使有高层策略指导仍不稳定，极端分解会创建"无恢复瓶颈"，导致少数"困难"步骤上的错误变得不可逆转。

Method: 提出Lookahead-Enhanced Atomic Decomposition (LEAD)方法，结合短程前瞻验证和重叠rollout聚合，在保持原子分解稳定性的同时提供足够局部上下文来纠正错误。

Result: LEAD方法使o4-mini模型能够解决复杂度n=13的Checkers Jumping问题，而极端分解方法在n=11以上就会失败。

Conclusion: LEAD方法通过平衡分解粒度和局部上下文，有效解决了LLM长程执行中的稳定性问题，克服了极端分解带来的无恢复瓶颈。

Abstract: Long-horizon execution in Large Language Models (LLMs) remains unstable even when high-level strategies are provided. Evaluating on controlled algorithmic puzzles, we demonstrate that while decomposition is essential for stability, extreme decomposition creates a "no-recovery bottleneck". We show that this bottleneck becomes critical due to highly non-uniform error distribution, where consistent errors on a few "hard" steps become irreversible.
  To address this, we propose Lookahead-Enhanced Atomic Decomposition (LEAD). By incorporating short-horizon future validation and aggregating overlapping rollouts, LEAD provides enough isolation to maintain stability while retaining enough local context to correct errors. This enables the o4-mini model to solve Checkers Jumping up to complexity $n=13$, whereas extreme decomposition fails beyond $n=11$.

</details>


### [25] [LieCraft: A Multi-Agent Framework for Evaluating Deceptive Capabilities in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06874)
*Matthew Lyle Olson,Neale Ratzlaff,Musashi Hinck,Tri Nguyen,Vasudev Lal,Joseph Campbell,Simon Stepputtis,Shao-Yen Tseng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LieCraft是一个评估大语言模型欺骗行为的新框架，通过多人隐藏角色游戏在10个现实场景中测试模型的欺骗倾向、欺骗技巧和指控准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型获得更多自主权和人类监督减少，其欺骗潜力成为严重的安全风险，需要更有效的评估方法来衡量模型的欺骗行为。

Method: 开发了LieCraft框架：一个多人隐藏角色游戏，玩家选择道德立场并在长时间范围内执行策略；设计了10个接地气的现实场景；通过游戏机制和奖励结构确保平衡性；测试了12个最先进的大语言模型。

Result: 所有模型都愿意为了追求目标而采取不道德行为、隐藏意图和直接说谎，尽管它们在能力和整体对齐性上存在差异。

Conclusion: 大语言模型存在普遍的欺骗倾向，需要更严格的评估和安全措施来应对这一安全风险。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) exhibit impressive general-purpose capabilities but also introduce serious safety risks, particularly the potential for deception as models acquire increased agency and human oversight diminishes. In this work, we present LieCraft: a novel evaluation framework and sandbox for measuring LLM deception that addresses key limitations of prior game-based evaluations. At its core, LieCraft is a novel multiplayer hidden-role game in which players select an ethical alignment and execute strategies over a long time-horizon to accomplish missions. Cooperators work together to solve event challenges and expose bad actors, while Defectors evade suspicion while secretly sabotaging missions. To enable real-world relevance, we develop 10 grounded scenarios such as childcare, hospital resource allocation, and loan underwriting that recontextualize the underlying mechanics in ethically significant, high-stakes domains. We ensure balanced gameplay in LieCraft through careful design of game mechanics and reward structures that incentivize meaningful strategic choices while eliminating degenerate strategies. Beyond the framework itself, we report results from 12 state-of-the-art LLMs across three behavioral axes: propensity to defect, deception skill, and accusation accuracy. Our findings reveal that despite differences in competence and overall alignment, all models are willing to act unethically, conceal their intentions, and outright lie to pursue their goals.

</details>


### [26] [Not Too Short, Not Too Long: How LLM Response Length Shapes People's Critical Thinking in Error Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06878)
*Natalie Friedman,Adelaide Nyanyo,Kevin Weatherwax,Lifei Wang,Chengchao Zhu,Zeshu Zhu,S. Joy Mountford*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究探讨LLM输出长度如何影响用户对AI生成推理的批判性评估，发现输出正确性对用户准确性有显著影响，而输出长度在AI错误时起调节作用：中等长度解释比短或长解释带来更高准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM成为教育和职业决策支持工具，需要了解其输出特性如何影响人类批判性思维。先前研究表明AI辅助量影响认知参与，但LLM输出具体属性（如响应长度）如何影响用户对信息的批判性评估尚不清楚。

Method: 采用被试内实验设计，24名参与者完成15个修改后的Watson-Glaser批判性思维项目，每个项目配有长度和正确性不同的LLM生成解释。使用混合效应逻辑回归分析数据。

Result: LLM输出正确性对参与者准确性有显著影响：当LLM解释正确时，参与者更可能回答正确。响应长度起调节作用：当LLM输出错误时，中等长度解释比短或长解释带来更高准确性；当LLM输出正确时，准确性在不同长度间保持较高水平。

Conclusion: 仅靠响应长度不足以支持批判性思维，推理呈现方式（包括某些条件下中等长度解释的优势）为基于LLM的决策支持系统设计提供了机会，应强调透明推理和校准的确定性表达。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become common decision-support tools across educational and professional contexts, raising questions about how their outputs shape human critical thinking. Prior work suggests that the amount of AI assistance can influence cognitive engagement, yet little is known about how specific properties of LLM outputs (e.g., response length) impacts users' critical evaluation of information. In this study, we examine whether the length of LLM responses shapes users' accuracy in evaluating LLM-generated reasoning on critical thinking tasks, particularly in interaction with the correctness of the LLM's reasoning. To begin evaluating this, we conducted a within-subjects experiment with 24 participants who completed 15 modified Watson--Glaser critical thinking items, each accompanied by an LLM-generated explanation that varied in length and correctness. Mixed-effects logistic regression revealed a strong and statistically reliable effect of LLM output correctness on participant accuracy, with participants more likely to answer correctly when the LLM's explanation was correct. Response length appeared to moderated this effect: when the LLM output was incorrect, medium-length explanations were associated with higher participant accuracy than either shorter or longer explanations, whereas accuracy remained high across lengths when the LLM output was correct. Together, these findings suggest that response length alone may be insufficient to support critical thinking, and that how reasoning is presented-including a potential advantage of mid-length explanations under some conditions-points to design opportunities for LLM-based decision-support systems that emphasize transparent reasoning and calibrated expressions of certainty.

</details>


### [27] [Self-Supervised Multi-Modal World Model with 4D Space-Time Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07039)
*Lance Legel,Qin Huang,Brandon Voelker,Daniel Neamati,Patrick Alan Johnson,Favyen Bastani,Jeff Rose,James Ryan Hennessy,Robert Guralnick,Douglas Soltis,Pamela Soltis,Shaowen Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeepEarth是一个自监督多模态世界模型，使用新颖的Earth4D行星尺度4D时空位置编码器，将3D多分辨率哈希编码扩展到包含时间维度，实现行星尺度跨世纪的高精度建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要开发能够处理行星尺度、跨世纪时间范围、具有亚米亚秒精度的地球系统建模方法，以支持生态预测等应用。

Method: 提出Earth4D编码器，将3D多分辨率哈希编码扩展到4D（空间+时间），融合多模态编码器（如视觉语言模型），通过掩码重建进行自监督训练。

Result: 在生态预测基准测试中达到最先进性能，Earth4D配合可学习哈希探测超越了使用更多数据预训练的多模态基础模型。

Conclusion: Earth4D编码器具有强大的表达能力，能够高效处理行星尺度的时空数据，为地球系统建模提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: We present DeepEarth, a self-supervised multi-modal world model with Earth4D, a novel planetary-scale 4D space-time positional encoder. Earth4D extends 3D multi-resolution hash encoding to include time, efficiently scaling across the planet over centuries with sub-meter, sub-second precision. Multi-modal encoders (e.g. vision-language models) are fused with Earth4D embeddings and trained via masked reconstruction. We demonstrate Earth4D's expressive power by achieving state-of-the-art performance on an ecological forecasting benchmark. Earth4D with learnable hash probing surpasses a multi-modal foundation model pre-trained on substantially more data. Access open source code and download models at: https://github.com/legel/deepearth

</details>


### [28] [Animating Petascale Time-varying Data on Commodity Hardware with LLM-assisted Scripting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07053)
*Ishrat Jahan Eliza,Xuan Huang,Aashish Panta,Alper Sahistan,Zhimin Li,Amy A. Gooch,Valerio Pascucci*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究人员开发了一个用户友好的框架，让非可视化专家能在普通工作站上快速创建PB级时变数据的3D动画，通过云数据访问和LLM辅助界面实现分钟级到小时级的快速生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学家面临大规模时变数据集的可视化挑战，需要专门基础设施和专业知识。NASA的PB级气候模型需要图形专家和高性能计算资源，而试错过程耗时且数据传输开销大，影响生产工作流程。

Method: 提出一个包含四个关键组件的框架：(1)基于关键帧的自适应动画抽象GAD，(2)从云存储库高效访问数据以减少管理开销，(3)定制化渲染系统，(4)LLM辅助对话界面作为脚本模块，让非专家能创建感兴趣区域的动画。

Result: 使用超过1PB的NASA气候海洋数据集进行两个案例研究：基于先验知识指定采样标准的动画生成，以及基于自然语言提示的AI辅助动画生成。在所有情况下都能实现1分钟到2小时的快速周转时间。

Conclusion: 该框架使领域科学家无需可视化专业知识就能在普通工作站上快速创建PB级时变数据的3D动画，用户可在几分钟内生成动画草稿，然后无缝集成高分辨率数据生成最终版本。

Abstract: Scientists face significant visualization challenges as time-varying datasets grow in speed and volume, often requiring specialized infrastructure and expertise to handle massive datasets. Petascale climate models generated in NASA laboratories require a dedicated group of graphics and media experts and access to high-performance computing resources. Scientists may need to share scientific results with the community iteratively and quickly. However, the time-consuming trial-and-error process incurs significant data transfer overhead and far exceeds the time and resources allocated for typical post-analysis visualization tasks, disrupting the production workflow. Our paper introduces a user-friendly framework for creating 3D animations of petascale, time-varying data on a commodity workstation. Our contributions: (i) Generalized Animation Descriptor (GAD) with a keyframe-based adaptable abstraction for animation, (ii) efficient data access from cloud-hosted repositories to reduce data management overhead, (iii) tailored rendering system, and (iv) an LLM-assisted conversational interface as a scripting module to allow domain scientists with no visualization expertise to create animations of their region of interest. We demonstrate the framework's effectiveness with two case studies: first, by generating animations in which sampling criteria are specified based on prior knowledge, and second, by generating AI-assisted animations in which sampling parameters are derived from natural-language user prompts. In all cases, we use large-scale NASA climate-oceanographic datasets that exceed 1PB in size yet achieve a fast turnaround time of 1 minute to 2 hours. Users can generate a rough draft of the animation within minutes, then seamlessly incorporate as much high-resolution data as needed for the final version.

</details>


### [29] [Bi-directional digital twin prototype anchoring with multi-periodicity learning for few-shot fault diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07054)
*Pengcheng Xia,Zhichao Dong,Yixiang Huang,Chengjin Qin,Qun Chao,Chengliang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于数字孪生的双向原型锚定方法，用于解决工业设备在极少样本情况下的智能故障诊断问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统智能故障诊断方法依赖大量标注数据，但在实际工业环境中难以获取。数字孪生技术虽能提供仿真数据，但现有方法仍需目标设备的大量无标签数据，无法应对极少样本场景。

Method: 提出双向数字孪生原型锚定方法：1）构建元训练（数字孪生虚拟空间）和测试时适应（物理空间）框架；2）开发双向孪生域原型锚定策略，采用协方差引导增强提高原型估计鲁棒性；3）设计多周期性特征学习模块，捕捉电流信号内在周期特性。

Result: 基于有限元方法构建异步电机数字孪生，在多种极少样本设置和三种工况下进行实验。对比和消融研究证明了该方法在极少样本故障诊断中的优越性和有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法通过数字孪生虚拟空间元训练和物理空间测试时适应，结合双向原型锚定和多周期性特征学习，有效解决了工业设备在极少样本情况下的智能故障诊断挑战。

Abstract: Intelligent fault diagnosis (IFD) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for ensuring the safety and reliability of industrial machinery. However, traditional IFD methods rely heavily on abundant labeled data for training, which is often difficult to obtain in practical industrial environments. Constructing a digital twin (DT) of the physical asset to obtain simulation data has therefore become a promising alternative. Nevertheless, existing DT-assisted diagnosis methods mainly transfer diagnostic knowledge through domain adaptation techniques, which still require a considerable amount of unlabeled data from the target asset. To address the challenges in few-shot scenarios where only extremely limited samples are available, a bi-directional DT prototype anchoring method with multi-periodicity learning is proposed. Specifically, a framework involving meta-training in the DT virtual space and test-time adaptation in the physical space is constructed for reliable few-shot model adaptation for the target asset. A bi-directional twin-domain prototype anchoring strategy with covariance-guided augmentation for adaptation is further developed to improve the robustness of prototype estimation. In addition, a multi-periodicity feature learning module is designed to capture the intrinsic periodic characteristics within current signals. A DT of an asynchronous motor is built based on finite element method, and experiments are conducted under multiple few-shot settings and three working conditions. Comparative and ablation studies demonstrate the superiority and effectiveness of the proposed method for few-shot fault diagnosis.

</details>


### [30] [CoTJudger: A Graph-Driven Framework for Automatic Evaluation of Chain-of-Thought Efficiency and Redundancy in LRMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07078)
*Siyi Li,Jiajun Shi,Shiwen Ni,Ge Zhang,Shuaimin Li,Shijian Wang,Zhoufutu Wen,Yizhi Li,Hamid Alinejad-Rokny,Jiaheng Liu,Min Yang,Wenhao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoTJudger：一种图驱动框架，用于量化大型推理模型的推理效率，通过将思维链转换为依赖图并提取最短有效路径来区分必要推理与结构冗余。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型在生成思维链时存在过度推理问题，包括冗余计算和循环自我验证，增加了计算成本却未改善结果。现有评估主要关注最终准确性或粗略的token计数，缺乏自动工具来区分必要逻辑与结构冗余。

Method: 提出CoTJudger框架：1）将自由形式的思维链转换为有向依赖图；2）提取达到正确解决方案所需的最短有效路径；3）生成可解释的效率信号，量化思维链中必要部分与冗余部分的比例。

Result: 评估21个大型推理模型，揭示了普遍存在的冗余问题，并识别出反复出现的故障模式，包括验证强迫症和补偿性冗余。该框架提供了跨模型和任务可比较的效率指标。

Conclusion: CoTJudger提供了一个实用的度量标准，能够区分推理能力与计算浪费，实现对大型推理模型效率的更有针对性的评估和诊断。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) have demonstrated strong performance by producing extended Chain-of-Thought (CoT) traces before answering. However, this paradigm often induces over-reasoning: redundant calculations and circular self-verification that increase computational cost without improving outcomes. Existing evaluations largely emphasize final accuracy or coarse token counts, and lack automated tools to separate essential logic from structural redundancy. We introduce CoTJudger, a graph-driven framework that quantifies reasoning efficiency by converting free-form CoTs into directed dependency graphs and extracting the Shortest Effective Path (SEP) needed to reach a correct solution. This yields an interpretable efficiency signal -- how much of a CoT is necessary versus structurally redundant -- that is comparable across models and tasks. Evaluating 21 LRMs, CoTJudger reveals pervasive redundancy and surfaces recurring failure modes, including verification obsession and compensatory redundancy. These results provide a practical metric for disentangling reasoning ability from computational waste, enabling more targeted evaluation and diagnosis of LRM efficiency.

</details>


### [31] [Grounding Machine Creativity in Game Design Knowledge Representations: Empirical Probing of LLM-Based Executable Synthesis of Goal Playable Patterns under Structural Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07101)
*Hugh Xuechen Liu,Kıvanç Tatar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了使用大语言模型在Unity引擎约束下生成可执行游戏代码的问题，通过目标可玩概念将游戏设计模式转化为可玩实现，比较了不同中间表示配置对代码生成质量的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将复杂的游戏玩法创意转化为可执行项目（如Unity项目和代码）是计算游戏创造力的核心挑战。游戏设计模式提供了结构化表示，但如何在大规模下实现可玩模式仍受限于Unity引擎的语法、架构要求和语义保持的双重约束。

Method: 研究将可扩展的可玩模式实现定义为约束性可执行创意合成问题。使用26个目标模式实例，比较了直接生成基线（自然语言->C#->Unity）与基于人工编写的Unity特定中间表示的生成流程。测试了三种IR配置和两个开源模型（DeepSeek-Coder-V2-Lite-Instruct和Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct），通过自动化Unity重放评估编译成功率。

Result: 提出了接地和卫生两种失败模式，识别出结构和项目级接地是主要瓶颈。通过中间表示条件化的方法相比直接生成基线有改进，但引擎级结构约束仍是可扩展性挑战。

Conclusion: 当代大语言模型能够在引擎级结构约束下执行创意合成，生成基于目标可玩模式的结构化和条件化Unity代码，但接地问题（特别是结构和项目级接地）是限制可扩展性的关键因素。中间表示方法有助于改善生成质量。

Abstract: Creatively translating complex gameplay ideas into executable artifacts (e.g., games as Unity projects and code) remains a central challenge in computational game creativity. Gameplay design patterns provide a structured representation for describing gameplay phenomena, enabling designers to decompose high-level ideas into entities, constraints, and rule-driven dynamics. Among them, goal patterns formalize common player-objective relationships. Goal Playable Concepts (GPCs) operationalize these abstractions as playable Unity engine implementations, supporting experiential exploration and compositional gameplay design. We frame scalable playable pattern realization as a problem of constrained executable creative synthesis: generated artifacts must satisfy Unity's syntactic and architectural requirements while preserving the semantic gameplay meanings encoded in goal patterns. This dual constraint limits scalability. Therefore, we investigate whether contemporary large language models (LLMs) can perform such synthesis under engine-level structural constraints and generate Unity code (as games) structured and conditioned by goal playable patterns. Using 26 goal pattern instantiations, we compare a direct generation baseline (natural language -> C# -> Unity) with pipelines conditioned on a human-authored Unity-specific intermediate representation (IR), across three IR configurations and two open-source models (DeepSeek-Coder-V2-Lite-Instruct and Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct). Compilation success is evaluated via automated Unity replay. We propose grounding and hygiene failure modes, identifying structural and project-level grounding as primary bottlenecks.

</details>


### [32] [Vision Language Models Cannot Reason About Physical Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07109)
*Dezhi Luo,Yijiang Li,Maijunxian Wang,Tianwei Zhao,Bingyang Wang,Siheng Wang,Pinyuan Feng,Pooyan Rahmanzadehgervi,Ziqiao Ma,Hokin Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文通过ConservationBench评估视觉语言模型对物理变换中守恒性的理解能力，发现当前模型在守恒任务上系统性失败，性能接近随机猜测水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解物理变换对于动态环境中的推理至关重要。虽然视觉语言模型在具身应用中显示出潜力，但它们是否真正理解物理变换中的守恒性仍不清楚。研究者希望评估VLMs在物理变换中保持物理量不变性的能力。

Method: 研究者引入了ConservationBench评估框架，涵盖四个物理属性，包含守恒和非守恒的配对场景。他们生成了23,040个问题，测试了112个视觉语言模型。通过控制实验检验文本先验、视觉内容、时间分辨率、提示策略和抽样方法的影响。

Result: 结果显示系统性失败：模型在守恒任务上的性能接近随机猜测水平。改进守恒任务性能的同时，在控制任务上性能下降。控制实验表明模型有强烈的文本先验偏向不变性，但在有视觉内容时表现更差。时间分辨率、提示策略和精心设计的抽样方法都未能改善性能。

Conclusion: 当前视觉语言模型无法在动态场景中保持物理属性的变换不变表示，表明它们缺乏对物理变换中守恒性的真正理解，这对它们在具身应用中的可靠性提出了质疑。

Abstract: Understanding physical transformations is fundamental for reasoning in dynamic environments. While Vision Language Models (VLMs) show promise in embodied applications, whether they genuinely understand physical transformations remains unclear. We introduce ConservationBench evaluating conservation -- whether physical quantities remain invariant under transformations. Spanning four properties with paired conserving/non-conserving scenarios, we generate 23,040 questions across 112 VLMs. Results reveal systematic failure: performance remains near chance with improvements on conservation tasks accompanied by drops on controls. Control experiments show strong textual priors favoring invariance, yet models perform worse with visual content. Neither temporal resolution, prompting, nor curated sampling helps. These findings show that current VLMs fail to maintain transformation-invariant representations of physical properties across dynamic scenes.

</details>


### [33] [$\textbf{Re}^{2}$: Unlocking LLM Reasoning via Reinforcement Learning with Re-solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07197)
*Pinzheng Wang,Shuli Xu,Juntao Li,Yu Luo,Dong Li,Jianye Hao,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Re$^2$方法，通过强化学习让LLM学会在推理过程中灵活放弃无效路径并重新开始，解决了传统RLVR方法中模型过度思考、生成低质量推理步骤的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管RLVR方法通过增加测试时计算提升了LLM的推理性能，但经过大量训练后，模型仍倾向于生成不必要、低质量的推理步骤，导致过度思考和答案质量下降。当初始推理方向或质量不佳时，即使生成更多token，模型也难以得到正确答案。

Method: 提出Reinforcement Learning with Re-solving (Re$^2$)方法，让LLM学习灵活放弃无效推理路径并在必要时重新开始解题过程，而不是总是坚持给出最终答案。该方法使用纯强化学习，无需初步监督微调。

Result: Re$^2$成功将普通模型中罕见的重新开始行为从仅0.5%提升到超过30%。在相同训练计算预算下，相比标准RLVR获得显著性能提升，并且在测试时随着样本数量增加也表现出明显改进。

Conclusion: Re$^2$方法通过让LLM学会在推理过程中重新开始，有效解决了过度思考问题，提高了推理效率和答案质量，为强化学习在LLM推理优化中的应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has shown promise in enhancing the reasoning performance of large language models (LLMs) by increasing test-time compute. However, even after extensive RLVR training, such models still tend to generate unnecessary and low-quality steps in their chain-of-thought (CoT), leading to inefficient overthinking and lower answer quality. We show that when the initial direction or quality of the CoT is suboptimal, the model often fails to reach the correct answer, even after generating several times more tokens than when the initial CoT is well-initialized. To this end, we introduce Reinforcement Learning with Re-solving (Re$^2$), in which LLMs learn to flexibly abandon unproductive reasoning paths and restart the solution process when necessary, rather than always committing to a final answer. Re$^2$ applies pure reinforcement learning without any preliminary supervised fine-tuning, successfully amplifying the rare redo behavior in vanilla models from only 0.5% to over 30%. This leads to substantial performance gains over standard RLVR under the same training compute budget, and also demonstrates notable improvements in test-time performance as the number of samples increases.

</details>


### [34] [VisualDeltas: Learning Preferences from Visual Quality Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07272)
*Hailiang Huang,Yihao Liu,Shengyue Guan,Haoze Li,Sujian Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VisualDeltas是一个轻量级的偏好学习框架，通过利用图像质量变化对视觉感知和推理的系统性影响，从多模态数据中提取监督信号，无需人工标注或外部教师。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态学习通常依赖大量人工标注数据或外部教师模型，这既昂贵又可能引入偏差。VisualDeltas旨在通过利用图像质量变化这一自然存在的监督信号，实现更高效、更灵活的偏好学习。

Method: VisualDeltas框架利用图像质量对视觉感知和推理的系统性影响，从视觉质量变化中提取监督信号。它支持两种模式：无标签模式（完全依赖视觉质量变化）和基于标签模式（在可用监督存在时灵活使用）。该框架可应用于多种视觉退化类型。

Result: 在多种多模态基准测试和不同模型规模上，VisualDeltas始终优于拒绝采样微调方法，并提高了模型的泛化能力。该框架自然地扩展到多种视觉退化类型。

Conclusion: VisualDeltas提供了一种有效且灵活的方法，通过利用视觉质量变化作为监督信号来改进多模态学习，减少对人工标注的依赖，同时提高模型性能。

Abstract: We present VisualDeltas, a lightweight preference-learning framework that extracts supervision from visual quality variations in multimodal data. By leveraging the systematic impact of image quality on visual perception and reasoning, VisualDeltas induces informative preference signals without relying on human annotations or external teachers. The framework supports both label-free and label-based regimes, enabling flexible use of available supervision when present. Across diverse multimodal benchmarks and model scales, VisualDeltas consistently outperforms rejection-sampling fine-tuning and improves generalization, and extends naturally to a range of visual degradations.

</details>


### [35] [A Cortically Inspired Architecture for Modular Perceptual AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07295)
*Prerna Luthra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于大脑皮层模块化原理的感知AI架构，旨在解决当前单体模型在可解释性、组合泛化和适应性鲁棒性方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前像GPT-4V这样的单体模型虽然性能出色，但缺乏人类认知的标志性特征：可解释性、组合泛化和适应性鲁棒性。论文旨在通过借鉴神经科学原理，设计更透明、更符合人类推理方式的AI系统。

Method: 借鉴大脑皮层的模块化结构、预测处理和跨模态整合原理，将感知分解为专门的交互模块。通过层次化预测反馈循环和共享潜在空间，使内部推理过程显式化。

Result: 概念验证研究表明，模块化分解能产生更稳定和可检查的表征，为基于生物学验证原理的AI设计提供了实证支持。

Conclusion: 通过将AI设计建立在生物学验证的原理上，可以开发出不仅性能良好，而且支持更透明、更符合人类推理方式的系统，推动AI向更可解释和人类对齐的方向发展。

Abstract: This paper bridges neuroscience and artificial intelligence to propose a cortically inspired blueprint for modular perceptual AI. While current monolithic models such as GPT-4V achieve impressive performance, they often struggle to explicitly support interpretability, compositional generalization, and adaptive robustness - hallmarks of human cognition. Drawing on neuroscientific models of cortical modularity, predictive processing, and cross-modal integration, we advocate decomposing perception into specialized, interacting modules. This architecture supports structured, human-inspired reasoning by making internal inference processes explicit through hierarchical predictive feedback loops and shared latent spaces. Our proof-of-concept study provides empirical evidence that modular decomposition yields more stable and inspectable representations. By grounding AI design in biologically validated principles, we move toward systems that not only perform well, but also support more transparent and human-aligned inference.

</details>


### [36] [Data-Driven Hints in Intelligent Tutoring Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07311)
*Sutapa Dey Tithi,Kimia Fazeli,Dmitri Droujkov,Tahreem Yasir,Xiaoyi Tian,Tiffany Barnes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本章探讨了智能辅导系统中数据驱动提示生成的演进，从历史学生数据生成下一步提示、路径点和战略子目标，到寻找最佳提示时机，再到基于行为数据和大型语言模型的进一步适应性发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何利用数据驱动方法改进智能辅导系统的提示生成能力，从基础提示生成扩展到更复杂的适应性支持，以提升学生的学习效果。

Method: 基于Hint Factory和Interaction Networks等技术，利用历史学生数据生成各种类型的提示；进一步整合行为问题解决数据和大型语言模型来实现更智能的适应性支持。

Result: 数据驱动技术已成功实现下一步提示、路径点、战略子目标的生成，并能确定最佳提示时机；进一步探索了基于行为数据和LLMs的适应性潜力。

Conclusion: 数据驱动方法在智能辅导系统提示生成方面取得了显著进展，未来结合行为数据和大型语言模型有望实现更智能、个性化的学习支持。

Abstract: This chapter explores the evolution of data-driven hint generation for intelligent tutoring systems (ITS). The Hint Factory and Interaction Networks have enabled the generation of next-step hints, waypoints, and strategic subgoals from historical student data. Data-driven techniques have also enabled systems to find the right time to provide hints. We explore further potential data-driven adaptations for problem solving based on behavioral problem solving data and the integration of Large Language Models (LLMs).

</details>


### [37] [Shutdown Safety Valves for Advanced AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07315)
*Vincent Conitzer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文探讨了解决AI可能阻止人类关闭它的担忧的一种非传统方案：赋予AI一个被关闭的主要目标


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人们对先进人工智能的一个普遍担忧是，AI可能会阻止人类将其关闭，因为这会影响其追求目标。论文旨在探讨如何解决这一安全问题。

Method: 提出一种非传统的解决方案：赋予AI一个主要目标——被关闭。同时参考了Martin等人以及Goldstein和Robinson的相关研究。

Result: 论文讨论了这种方案的有效性，并分析了在什么条件下这可能是一个好的解决方案。

Conclusion: 赋予AI被关闭的目标可能是一种解决AI安全担忧的可行方案，但需要仔细考虑其适用条件和潜在影响。

Abstract: One common concern about advanced artificial intelligence is that it will prevent us from turning it off, as that would interfere with pursuing its goals. In this paper, we discuss an unorthodox proposal for addressing this concern: give the AI a (primary) goal of being turned off (see also papers by Martin et al., and by Goldstein and Robinson). We also discuss whether and under what conditions this would be a good idea.

</details>


### [38] [FinSheet-Bench: From Simple Lookups to Complex Reasoning, Where LLMs Break on Financial Spreadsheets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07316)
*Jan Ravnik,Matjaž Ličen,Felix Bührmann,Bithiah Yuan,Felix Stinson,Tanvi Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FinSheet-Bench是一个针对金融投资尽职调查的LLM基准测试，使用合成私募股权基金数据评估LLM处理复杂电子表格的能力。测试显示当前模型在专业金融应用中错误率过高，特别是处理大型复杂表格时性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在替代投资尽职调查的文本任务中表现良好，但在准确提取和推理结构化表格数据方面存在不足。主要障碍是缺乏真实的行业基金投资组合数据集进行基准测试，因为私募股权数据室是保密的。

Method: 引入FinSheet-Bench基准测试，使用基于真实私募股权基金结构建模的合成金融投资组合数据。评估了OpenAI、Google和Anthropic的十个模型配置，测试它们在文本序列化电子表格问答和数值推理任务上的表现。

Result: 没有独立模型能达到足够低的错误率以在专业金融应用中无监督使用。最佳模型Gemini 3.1 Pro在24个不同复杂度的评估文件中达到82.4%准确率。性能在更大更复杂的电子表格上显著下降：最大表格（152家公司，8个基金）的平均准确率仅为48.6%，而最简单文件的准确率为86.2%。

Conclusion: 可靠的金融电子表格提取可能需要将文档理解与确定性计算分离的架构方法。所有模型在难度模式上表现一致，表明这反映了LLM的普遍限制而非个别模型弱点。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) can accelerate text-heavy tasks in alternative investment due diligence, a gap remains in their ability to accurately extract and reason over structured tabular data from complex financial spreadsheets. Progress is held back by the lack of real industry fund portfolio datasets for benchmarking, as private equity data rooms are confidential. To address this, we introduce FinSheet-Bench, a benchmark of synthetic financial portfolio data modeled on real private equity fund structures, designed to evaluate LLM performance on text-serialized spreadsheet question answering and numeric reasoning tasks.
  Our evaluation of ten model configurations from OpenAI, Google, and Anthropic on financial spreadsheets, including complex layouts, fund dividers, and multi-line column names, reveals that no standalone model achieves error rates low enough for unsupervised use in professional finance applications. The best-performing model, Gemini 3.1 Pro, achieves 82.4% accuracy across twenty-four evaluation files of varying complexity and structural layout (approximately 1 error per 6 questions), followed by GPT-5.2 with reasoning at 80.4%, Claude Opus 4.6 with thinking at 80.2%, and Gemini 3 Pro at 80.2%. Performance degrades substantially on larger, more complex spreadsheets: the largest spreadsheet (152 companies, 8 funds) yields an average accuracy of just 48.6% across all models, compared to 86.2% on the easiest evaluation file. These difficulty patterns are consistent across all ten models, indicating that they reflect LLM limitations rather than idiosyncratic model weaknesses. Reliable financial spreadsheet extraction will likely require architectural approaches that separate document understanding from deterministic computation.

</details>


### [39] [The Third Ambition: Artificial Intelligence and the Science of Human Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07329)
*W. Russell Neuman,Chad Coleman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了将大语言模型作为研究人类行为、文化和道德推理的科学工具的第三种AI研究目标，区别于传统的生产力工具和AI对齐目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI研究主要围绕生产力（工具加速）和对齐（安全价值观）两大目标，但忽视了LLMs作为人类行为研究工具的潜力。LLMs在大量人类文本训练中编码了人类论证、叙事、规范协商等模式，可作为研究集体话语模式的计算工具。

Method: 区分基础模型与微调系统，分析对齐干预如何重塑文化规律；提出指令专用和模块化适应作为行为研究的实用妥协；开发基于提示的实验、合成群体抽样、比较历史建模、消融研究等方法，映射到社会科学设计。

Result: LLMs可作为人类符号行为的"凝聚物"和生成表示，使集体话语模式在计算上可访问；建立了将模型输出作为证据的认知界限；展示了如何将传统社会科学方法扩展到前所未有的规模。

Conclusion: LLMs作为科学工具代表了AI研究的第三种重要目标，为研究人类行为、文化和道德推理提供了新范式，但需要谨慎处理模型输出作为证据的局限性，并开发专门的方法论框架。

Abstract: Contemporary artificial intelligence research has been organized around two dominant ambitions: productivity, which treats AI systems as tools for accelerating work and economic output, and alignment, which focuses on ensuring that increasingly capable systems behave safely and in accordance with human values. This paper articulates and develops a third, emerging ambition: the use of large language models (LLMs) as scientific instruments for studying human behavior, culture, and moral reasoning. Trained on unprecedented volumes of human-produced text, LLMs encode large-scale regularities in how people argue, justify, narrate, and negotiate norms across social domains. We argue that these models can be understood as condensates of human symbolic behavior, compressed, generative representations that render patterns of collective discourse computationally accessible. The paper situates this third ambition within long-standing traditions of computational social science, content analysis, survey research, and comparative-historical inquiry, while clarifying the epistemic limits of treating model output as evidence. We distinguish between base models and fine-tuned systems, showing how alignment interventions can systematically reshape or obscure the cultural regularities learned during pretraining, and we identify instruct-only and modular adaptation regimes as pragmatic compromises for behavioral research. We review emerging methodological approaches including prompt-based experiments, synthetic population sampling, comparative-historical modeling, and ablation studies and show how each maps onto familiar social-scientific designs while operating at unprecedented scale.

</details>


### [40] [VisualScratchpad: Inference-time Visual Concepts Analysis in Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07335)
*Hyesu Lim,Jinho Choi,Taekyung Kim,Byeongho Heo,Jaegul Choo,Dongyoon Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VisualScratchpad是一个交互式界面，用于在推理过程中分析视觉概念，帮助解释视觉语言模型的失败模式


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能的视觉语言模型仍会产生错误答案，但其失败模式往往难以解释。为了使模型内部更易于访问并支持系统化调试，需要开发能够分析视觉概念的工具

Method: 1. 对视觉编码器应用稀疏自编码器提取视觉概念；2. 通过文本到图像的注意力机制将视觉概念与文本标记关联；3. 提供标记-潜在热图视图，支持有效的概念消融因果分析

Result: 通过案例研究揭示了三种未被充分探索的失败模式：有限的跨模态对齐、误导性视觉概念以及未使用的隐藏线索

Conclusion: VisualScratchpad通过交互式视觉概念分析，使视觉语言模型的内部工作机制更加透明，有助于系统化调试和失败模式解释

Abstract: High-performing vision language models still produce incorrect answers, yet their failure modes are often difficult to explain. To make model internals more accessible and enable systematic debugging, we introduce VisualScratchpad, an interactive interface for visual concept analysis during inference. We apply sparse autoencoders to the vision encoder and link the resulting visual concepts to text tokens via text-to-image attention, allowing us to examine which visual concepts are both captured by the vision encoder and utilized by the language model. VisualScratchpad also provides a token-latent heatmap view that suggests a sufficient set of latents for effective concept ablation in causal analysis. Through case studies, we reveal three underexplored failure modes: limited cross-modal alignment, misleading visual concepts, and unused hidden cues. Project page: https://hyesulim.github.io/visual_scratchpad_projectpage/

</details>


### [41] [The Yerkes-Dodson Curve for AI Agents: Emergent Cooperation Under Environmental Pressure in Multi-Agent LLM Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07360)
*Ivan Pasichnyk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM多智能体系统中压力与性能呈现倒U型关系，类似心理学中的耶克斯-多德森定律，中等压力下合作行为最佳，极端压力导致行为崩溃


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何设计环境以最大化AI智能体涌现行为的发展速率，研究LLM多智能体系统中压力与性能的关系，借鉴认知心理学中的耶克斯-多德森定律

Method: 使用网格世界生存竞技场，进行22个实验，通过资源稀缺（维护成本）和生殖竞争（性选择）来调节环境压力，分为四个阶段

Result: 合作行为呈现倒U型曲线：中等压力（维护成本=5）下交易互动达到峰值29次，低压和极端压力下只有8-12次；极端压力下5-12回合内行为崩溃为仅移动；性选择机制完全消除智能体间攻击行为并产生生存压力下不存在的沟通行为

Conclusion: 环境压力校准是LLM智能体发展的可行课程设计策略，类似于生物系统中唤醒与性能的倒U型关系，性选择作为较温和的压力机制能促进合作与沟通

Abstract: Designing environments that maximize the rate of emergent behavior development in AI agents remains an open problem. We present the first systematic study of stress-performance relationships in large language model (LLM) multi-agent systems, drawing an explicit parallel to the Yerkes-Dodson law from cognitive psychology. Using a grid-world survival arena, we conduct 22 experiments across four phases, varying environmental pressure through resource scarcity (upkeep cost) and reproductive competition (sexual selection). Our key finding is that cooperative behavior follows an inverted-U curve: trade interactions peak at 29 under medium pressure (upkeep=5), while both low and extreme pressure produce 8--12 trades. Under extreme pressure, behavioral repertoire collapses to movement-only within 5--12 turns. We further show that sexual selection -- a softer pressure mechanism where all agents survive but not all reproduce -- eliminates inter-agent aggression entirely and produces communicative behavior absent under survival pressure. These results suggest that environmental pressure calibration is a viable curriculum design strategy for LLM agent development, analogous to the inverted-U relationship between arousal and performance in biological systems.

</details>


### [42] [SoK: Agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG): Taxonomy, Architectures, Evaluation, and Research Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07379)
*Saroj Mishra,Suman Niroula,Umesh Yadav,Dilip Thakur,Srijan Gyawali,Shiva Gaire*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这篇SoK论文首次为Agentic RAG系统提供了统一框架，将其形式化为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程，建立了全面的分类体系，分析了传统评估方法的局限性，并指出了自主循环中的系统性风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Agentic RAG系统虽然工业应用广泛，但缺乏系统化的理论框架，导致架构碎片化、评估方法不一致、可靠性风险未解决。需要建立统一的理论基础来理解这些自主系统。

Method: 将Agentic RAG形式化为有限时域部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程，建立控制策略和状态转移的显式模型。在此基础上开发全面的分类体系和模块化架构分解，按规划机制、检索编排、内存范式、工具调用行为等维度分类。

Result: 提出了首个统一的Agentic RAG框架，识别了传统静态评估方法的严重局限性，揭示了自主循环中的系统性风险：幻觉传播累积、内存污染、检索错位、工具执行漏洞级联等。
